Escort #p2022005# to #m921120500# where #o9300281# is sealed to prevent his resurrection. - #eTime Limit: 30 min - #ePlayers: 3 - 6 - #eNote: You can receive additional EXP by completing this quest after clearing the #rRobeira's Proposal quest.
